2522
Researchers propose GradMix, a model-agnostic method for multi-source transfer learning across domains and tasks in a semi-supervised setting, leveraging off-the-shelf labeled datasets to improve model performance.
-----
-----
2163
Researchers argue that the generalization performance of linear graph embedding methods is due to the small norm of embedding vectors, not the dimension constraint, and provide both theoretical and empirical evidence to support this claim, highlighting the importance of proper norm regularization.
-----
-----
226
This research introduces Symplectic ODE-Net (SymODEN), a deep learning framework that infers physical system dynamics from observed state trajectories, incorporating physics-informed bias for better generalization and interpretability.
-----
-----
1133
Researchers questioned the validity of existing saliency methods for explaining deep neural networks, as they failed simple sanity checks. A new approach, "competition for pixels," is proposed to improve these methods.
-----
-----
995
Researchers propose a new approach to generative adversarial networks (GANs), called Relativistic GANs (RGANs), where the discriminator estimates the probability that real data is more realistic than fake data, improving stability and quality of generated samples.
-----
-----
895
This paper introduces simple_rl, an open-source Python library for reinforcement learning experiments, aiming to simplify the process with a focus on reproducibility and seamless execution.
-----
-----
492
Researchers propose a new approach, Optimistic Pessimistically Initialised Q-Learning (OPIQ), to improve exploration in reinforcement learning by augmenting Q-value estimates with count-derived bonuses to ensure optimism in both action selection and bootstrapping.
-----
-----
2162
Researchers argue that the generalization performance of linear graph embedding methods is not due to dimensionality constraint, but rather the small norm of embedding vectors, supported by theoretical and empirical evidence.
-----
-----
364
Researchers propose Nucleus Sampling, a method to improve text generation from language models by truncating the unreliable tail of the probability distribution, resulting in higher quality and diverse text.
-----
-----
399
Researchers propose a modular framework for group convolutional neural networks (G-CNNs) that can handle arbitrary Lie groups, enabling localized, atrous, and deformable convolutions.
-----
-----
836
A new algorithm, Anticipated Reweighted Truncated Backpropagation (ARTBP), is introduced to address the limitations of Truncated Backpropagation Through Time (truncated BPTT), which favors short-term dependencies and lacks convergence guarantees.
-----
-----
1025
This research introduces a new method for training Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) using the Wasserstein-2 metric proximal on generators, improving training speed and stability.

2523
Researchers propose GradMix, a model-agnostic method for multi-source transfer learning across domains and tasks in a semi-supervised setting, leveraging off-the-shelf labeled datasets to improve target task performance.
-----
-----
454
Researchers used automatic search techniques to discover a new activation function, Swish (f(x) = x * sigmoid(beta * x), which outperforms the widely-used Rectified Linear Unit (ReLU) on deeper models, improving top-1 classification accuracy by 0.6-0.9% on challenging datasets.
-----
-----
2787
Researchers propose a model using attention layers to improve learning heuristics for combinatorial optimization problems, achieving near-optimal results for the Travelling Salesman Problem and outperforming baselines on other problems like Vehicle Routing and Orienteering Problem.
-----
-----
812
Researchers implemented text classification and sentiment analysis on mobile devices using Power-efficient CNN Domain Specific Accelerator (CNN-DSA) chips, achieving state-of-the-art results with low power consumption and memory-efficient network compression.
-----
-----
647
Researchers introduce Colored Local Iterative Procedure (CLIP), a graph neural network that uses colors to disambiguate node attributes, improving the expressive power of Message Passing Neural Networks (MPNNs) and achieving state-of-the-art results on graph classification datasets.
-----
-----
1995
Researchers propose Neural Logic Inductive Learning (NLIL), a framework that enables machine learning systems to make explainable decisions by learning first-order logic rules from data, outperforming state-of-the-art models in efficiency and scalability.
-----
-----
453
Researchers used automatic search techniques to discover a new activation function, Swish (f(x) = x * sigmoid(beta * x), which outperforms the widely-used Rectified Linear Unit (ReLU) on deeper models across various datasets, improving accuracy by up to 0.9%.
-----
-----
633
Researchers improved the variational auto-encoder (VAE) framework by incorporating spatial information into the stochastic layers, achieving state-of-the-art results on various image datasets, including MNIST, OMNIGLOT, CIFAR10, and ImageNet.
